A false return from E6 on a first registration (the `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` overflow, `emitter_subjects_overflow` increments, EM AC-27) is a **budget defect** (the subject reads dark) and a lint or acceptance-criterion failure, never a gameplay state; a false return on an already-registered ref is not. Horror reads `lux(ref)` and `lit`; it calls no `probe` at MVP (Emitter EM-24 anticipates Horror pathing probes; HE-25 pathing needs none, so Horror consumes none of `MAX_PROBES_PER_TICK` 4, shared with Interaction (Attention makes no `probe()` call and does not read it, AT-4), and "Horror spams probes: only `probes_allowed` are served", EM:723). `HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` 2 (one active plus one transient during ingress), range 1 to 12; the Emitter's "placeholder 12" (EM:229) is a ceiling Horror owns, not the plan; I-CAPS (d) holds (2 + actors 4 ≤ `MAX_MOVING_SUBJECTS` 16). Interaction's `H` (REG `subject_budget`) and Derelict Authoring's `HORRORS` now quote this registered number, `HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` 2 (C-HE-4, closed 2026-10-27). *Cites.* EM-21, EM-24, EM:229.

**HE-35. L3 light disturbance: published state, never a call.** *Rule.* `horror.view.disturbances[]` lists `{emitter_id, strength_pm, start_T, dur_ticks}` for emitters of kind FIXTURE and NODE_LANTERN in the horror's body within `L3_RADIUS_PX` of its foot point, in **any phase**. **Never** a BEAM, a BEACON, a FAULT_GLOW, any DEPLOYABLE (glowsticks), and **never the EMERGENCY fixture subkind** (`fx_cryo_lamp`): this is Horror's answer to Lighting's open questions 15 and 22, "EMERGENCY stays exempt". A disturbance starts on an eligible lamp at a run when a pure hash `h(emitter_id, run_n, HE_HASH_SEED) mod 1000 < L3_START_PM` and `T >= last_end_T + L3_MIN_GAP_TICKS` (the ruled `L3_MIN_GAP_FRAMES` 120 is stored in ticks because a SIM block cannot count presentation frames, H9; one tick equals one frame at the nominal 60 fps and Lighting maps it). `strength_pm` lies in `[L3_STRENGTH_LO_PM, 1000 − MOD_FLOOR_PM]` and `dur_ticks` in `[L3_DUR_LO, L3_DUR_HI]`, both from the same pure hash (HE-F11); at most `L3_MAX_ACTIVE` lamps at once. A disturbance changes a lamp's **output only**: no disturbance touches a beam, the beacon, a fault glow or a glowstick, or changes a real light's radius, position or colour (Lighting's submitter contract, LO OQ 15). Horror never writes an Emitter field, never feeds the query and never calls Lighting (KRN:117; LO:424 must-repeat verbatim: disturbance is never applied to a BEAM, BEACON, FAULT_GLOW or any DEPLOYABLE, never an Emitter field, never an input to the query; horrors never occlude and emit nothing). The verb "flicker" never describes a fixture (LO-20). Disturbances are persisted (save-equivalence). A lamp enumeration near a point is needed (`emitters_near(body_id, px, r)`, name provisional): *(provisional — Emitter/Lighting read owed, C-HE-15)*. *Why.* The Pillar 4(a) "light disturbance" footprint: local and unsynchronised, which separates it from the tell (global, beat-locked, AT:1184); the player's test is "is it everywhere on the beat, or one place?". *Cites.* LO-18, LO-19, LO-20, LO:424, LO OQ 15 and 22.

**HE-36. No noise push.** *Rule.* Horror calls `push_stimulus` **never**. Audio facts stay published (HE-37). *Why.* A horror that feeds the ledger can hold BREACH open past `CLEAR_HOLD_TICKS` (the C8 spiral) and puts a lever outside the player's restraint (Pillar 3). AC-AT-95 refuses any `push_stimulus` caller other than the Controller and Interaction (a Horror double is refused), so Horror is not a declared caller (registry `push_stimulus`). *Cites.* AT:373, AC-AT-95.

#### Group H — Information, rules and reward

**HE-37. Audio and presentation facts, not sounds.** *Rule.* `horror.view` publishes, per entity: `entity_id`, `type_id`, `state` (LURK, STALK, REACH, STRIKE, RETREAT, TRANSIT), `pos_px`, `body_id`, `section_id`, `locked_pt` and `reach_end_T` (REACH), `strike_T` and the seats hit (STRIKE), `transit_until_T`, ingress and egress events with their tick, `disturbances[]`, the mark ring, and diagnostic counters (`horror_footprints_withheld`, `horror_marks_withheld`, `horror_ingress_pending`, `horror_chill_withheld_*`, E6 refusals). These `horror_*` names are **frozen** for the Debug Overlay, which reads them by channel and restates none as a fact (DO P-11). Adaptive Audio owns every sound, mix and cue name; the Horror sub-tell family is **disjoint** from Attention's tell family, the Graph loop sounds and Sanity's hallucination family (Audio AA-6 (the identity table), Sanity R28); phantom horrors use the Filter and Sanity audio classes, **never the Graph witness acoustic classes**. Horror never mutes, ducks or filters anything. A `tell_id` and `type_id` enumeration is kept stable and enumerable so the Knowledge Codex (Vertical Slice) can target one. Designed (`adaptive-audio-and-tells.md`, AA-28, AA-29). *Cites.* AT:1204, IDX:41.

**HE-38. No combat, no reward.** *Rule.* No horror HP, no kill state, no loot, no XP, no kill counter, no hit feedback for tool use, no player damage output, no hit-stop, no screen shake, no damage number, no threat bar and no proximity indicator. HORROR subjects take no verb at MVP: any forced command is `INVALID_TARGET` (IA-39, AC-IA-113). The beam only reveals (HE-13). "HORROR subjects take no verb" holds at MVP only; the Vertical Slice B `repel` declaration fields and their parity precondition (IA-31) are recorded in Open Questions row 27 only. The data-file lint of HE-5 and a repository scan for the feedback words in the vocabulary list are acceptance criteria. *Why.* The anti-pillar "not a combat game"; "horrors are avoided and survived, not farmed". *Cites.* IA:288, IA:468, PC-12, FT:98.

**HE-39. Suppressant: a data-dormant interface, with a conflict surfaced.** *Rule.* **No suppressant effect ships at MVP**, per AT-19 (designed, CD-approved: "no effect verb ships at MVP ... a Vertical Slice addendum"; a suppressant that aborts a tell would "make restraint an item"). Horror declares only the dormant interface `repel(origin, T)` with `REPEL_RADIUS_PX` 192 and `REPEL_TICKS` 600 [PLACEHOLDER]: any record within the radius would go RETREAT for the window, begin no REACH or STRIKE, never die, drop nothing, never touch Attention's level or tell. When the effect verb ships, its registrant reads the suppressant's `OUT_OF_CHARGES` through Suit's `tool_charge(player_id, def_id)` (SE read table); Horror never stores or writes a charge. Its acceptance criteria read **NOT ASSESSED at MVP, never PASS**. **Conflict C-HE-6 (for the creative-director):** the start kit carries `tool_suppressant` with `SUPPRESSANT_START_CHARGES` 3 and no effect (SE:201, IDB:249), a Pillar 1 problem of its own (a tool whose use changes nothing in the world is a lie the sane game tells). The creative-director's position is that the MVP start kit must not contain `tool_suppressant`: owed rows to Suit and the Item DB. If the user later reverses AT-19 the effect is a one-owner change and `[CUT-FIRST]`. **Gate condition (BLOCKING; creative-director position confirmed at the CD-GDD-ALIGN gate):** the owed rows to Suit & Equipment and the Item & Module Database that remove `tool_suppressant` from the MVP start kit and MVP item list must land before implementation; the item cannot ship inert (AC-HE-124). *Cites.* AT:243, SE:201, IDB:249.

**HE-40. Rig presenter, Sanity and the Perception Filter.** *Rule.* A render-side rig presenter takes `(pose_stream, type_id)` and an optional `leak` input that **only the Filter may set**; Horror feeds it real poses with `leak` 0; the Filter (Vertical Slice) may feed phantom or crewmate-driven poses (ART:594 "0 extra frames"; class (c) is driven by the crewmate's true velocity and true beam). Conformance to the Pipeline's row 14: Y-sort with the core's foot row as sort origin, 15-degree quantised segment rotation, pose stepped every 5th tick, `present_time` is not the rig's clock (PR-9), the presenter derives a five-tick interpolation alpha from the kernel's one-tick `tick_alpha` (owed to the Pipeline, C-HE-14), and horror presentation never writes the camera (C1, C9; the jolt API refuses it). Three canonical poses (rest, reach, move) pass the silhouette test: no closed circle, no bilateral symmetry, no four-limb pairs, no emission, iridescence band only under light. A rig instance with no Horror ref in `horror.view` and `leak` 0 is a lint error. Sanity's rulings decide what Horror owes: keep every real entity registered (E6) with a true anchor and publish true positions in `horror.view`; Horror computes no exposure, owns neither `HORROR_NEAR_R` (called `HORROR_SEEN_R` in the Sanity rulings; renamed in the Sanity draft, C-HE-32) nor `EXP_HORROR`, and holds no sanity reference. -> ADR candidate (3). *(provisional — Sanity designed in this run; Perception Filter undesigned.)* *Cites.* PIPE:303, PCI row 16, ART:594, SN-22.

#### Group I — State, determinism and budget

**HE-41. Block contents and restore.** *Rule.* Persisted per record: an in-block `entity_id` counter, `type_id`, `body_id`, `section_id`, `pos_px`, residue, `last_octant`, `acc_mpx`, `state`, `state_enter_T`, `locked_pt`, `reach_end_T`, `cooldown_until_T`, `transit_until_T`, `last_seen_tell_epoch`, `strikes_epoch`, `gouges_epoch`, `ruptures_epoch`, `chill_epoch_dj`, `chill_deposits_epoch`, `epoch_id`. Block-level: the mark ring and `mark_id` counter, the `"horror.tell"` counter, `fp_window` and `fp_count`, the active disturbance list and the `last_end_T` map (bounded by `L3_MAX_TRACKED`), `lurk_dwell_index`. **Not persisted:** the target, any threat of any kind (none exists), the path cache. Restore is two-phase with a closed set `HORROR_RESTORE_*` (`SCHEMA`, `RANGE`, `STATE_INCOHERENT`, `SECTION_UNKNOWN`, `RNG_COUNTER`, `MARK_RING`); an out-of-range value is a hard failure, never clamped (SL-15). A restore **into REACH** keeps the full remaining telegraph (never shortened; mirrors AT E-51) and re-checks the predicate; if it no longer holds, RETREAT with no strike. A restore into TRANSIT with `transit_until_T` in the past is legal (arrive next run); a TRANSIT record with a registered subject, or a non-TRANSIT record without a legal section, is `STATE_INCOHERENT`. All explicit dirty keys; plain data only, no `StringName` keys or `Vector2i`; restore is idempotent. -> ADR candidate (5). *Cites.* SL-15, SL:221-232, AT E-51.

**HE-42. Determinism.** *Rule.* All simulation randomness is `rng_derive(world_seed, "horror.tell", counter)` with the counter in the block, used for the REACH length only. Mark seeds, LURK waypoint choice and disturbance timing are pure integer hashes of ids, the run index and `HE_HASH_SEED`. The same seed and inputs give the same strike lengths, positions, marks and `state_hash` (D.12). No wall clock and no global RNG (H9). -> ADR candidate (4): a shared pure-integer presentation hash with Lighting LO-19. *Cites.* KRN D.10, D.12, H9.

**HE-43. Budget.** *Rule.* Target cost of `horror.step` is `HORROR_STEP_BUDGET_MS` 0.15 ms P99 per run (a share of the kernel's 2.0 ms P3 target; GDScript operation cost is unsourced and is a spike parameter; the kernel's `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 is a logging threshold). Per run: at most the number of seats in `lux` reads (up to 4), at most 5 `solid_in` queries, at most `PATH_EXPANSIONS_MAX` expansions on a cache miss, 0 probes, at most `MARKS_PER_RUN_MAX` mark inserts, at most `MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` M17 calls, at most one `apply_suit_damage` per seat. *Cites.* REG `STEP_BUDGET_MS`.

**HE-44. Reserved formula and constant index.** The Formulas section writes these ids; rules above cite them.

| Id | Subject | Cited by |
|---|---|---|
| HE-F1 | Per-window footprint budget (`HE_FOOTPRINT_BUDGET`, `MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN`) | HE-29 |
| HE-F2 | Strike geometry and walk-out time | HE-14, HE-15 |
| HE-F3 | Integrity after k strikes (the `STRIKE_BP` two-bound) | HE-15 |
| HE-F4 | CHILL cumulative bound | HE-31 |
| HE-F5 | REACH reaction bound (`UNFOLD_LO`) | HE-4, HE-14 |
| HE-F6 | Speed cadence and the escape invariant | HE-24 |
| HE-F7 | Mark ring eviction and lifetime | HE-30 |
| HE-F8 | Target selection from `lux_pm` | HE-13 |
| HE-F9 | Ingress timing and entry choice | HE-20 |
| HE-F10 | Path expansion bound | HE-25 |

| HE-F13 | Tell composition timeline | HE-9 |

### States and Transitions

The record's `state` takes one of six values: `LURK`, `STALK`, `REACH`, `STRIKE`, `RETREAT`, `TRANSIT`. The first five are the cycle; `TRANSIT` is a **sixth value, additive** to the five the audio facts need (C-HE-17): relocation through a hull needs a visible place in the machine and has no position. "Permitted act" is what the state may do to the world. Dormancy (HE-21) is not a state: a record whose body is unloaded is frozen in whatever state it holds, with no step.

#### State table

| State | Entry (from, guard) | Phases it lives in | Permitted act | Registered subject | Motion | Exit (first match wins) | Who may cause the transition |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **LURK** | boot or default; STALK on a TELL abort; RETREAT when the phase is CALM or TELL and the record is not returning; TRANSIT arrival while the phase is CALM | CALM (any other phase only transiently) | marks, CHILL, disturbance dips; no RUPTURE, no GOUGE, no suit damage; never within `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` of a living seat | yes | `V_CREEP_MPX` between waypoints; dwell `LURK_DWELL_TICKS` | `attention.view.phase` = TELL with a new `tell_epoch` → STALK (locus body is its body) or TRANSIT (another body); return due → TRANSIT; body unloaded → frozen | **Attention's phase only** (HE-7); never the player's light, noise or distance |
| **STALK** | LURK on TELL entry; RETREAT when the cooldown has elapsed in BREACH; TRANSIT arrival in TELL or BREACH | TELL, BREACH | marks, CHILL, disturbance. TELL: the standoff holds, no harm. BREACH: may begin a REACH under HE-14 | yes | TELL `V_CREEP_MPX`; BREACH `V_HUNT_MPX` | TELL abort (phase back to CALM) → LURK; BREACH and HE-14 (a) to (e) true → REACH; SUBSIDING → RETREAT; no lit seat → the anchor becomes the target (still STALK) | `attention.view` fields and the Emitter `lit` flag; the abort is Attention's |
| **REACH** | STALK in BREACH when the predicate holds at begin | BREACH (resolves in BREACH or SUBSIDING) | lays the touch-down SMEAR at P at REACH start; publishes `locked_pt` and `reach_end_T`; no harm yet; rooted | yes | none | `T >= reach_end_T` and the predicate holds → STRIKE; the predicate false at that run, or the phase CALM, or a restore with the predicate false → RETREAT (fizzle: no harm, no hairlines, no GOUGE or RUPTURE, no cooldown; the touch-down smear stays); `SUBSIDE_CANCELS_REACH` 1 and SUBSIDING → RETREAT (fizzle) | the predicate (Attention's `exogenous_permitted`); the horror's own clock |
| **STRIKE** | REACH resolve | BREACH or SUBSIDING | on the entry run only: `apply_suit_damage` per HE-15; GOUGE and RUPTURE per HE-16; hairline mark; publishes `strike_T` and the seats hit | yes | none (the snap pose) | after `STRIKE_HOLD_RUNS` runs → RETREAT | the horror's own clock |
| **RETREAT** | STRIKE; a fizzle; SUBSIDING entry; (Vertical Slice) a `repel` | BREACH, SUBSIDING, then CALM | marks only | yes | `V_CREEP_MPX` away from every living seat | `T >= cooldown_until_T` and BREACH → STALK; CALM or TELL and not returning → LURK; return due → TRANSIT | Attention's phase; the horror's own clock |
| **TRANSIT** | TELL entry with `body_of(locus_section_id)` not its own; return due (`RETURN_DELAY_TICKS`); its section lost to a cut (HE-22) | any | none; no position, no marks (the egress and ingress marks are laid at the segments, not by the traveller), not registered | no | none | `T >= transit_until_T` and a legal entry (HE-20) → STALK (TELL or BREACH), LURK (CALM) or RETREAT (SUBSIDING), with E6 and an ingress mark | Attention's locus; Layout for legality |

#### Phase view (state against phase: the single table a reviewer tests against)

| Phase | LURK | STALK | REACH | STRIKE | RETREAT | TRANSIT |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| CALM | resident | → LURK (after an abort) | fizzle | n/a | → LURK | arrives as LURK |
| TELL | → STALK | creep, standoff holds, no harm | cannot begin | n/a | → LURK | arrives as STALK |
| BREACH | n/a | hunt; may begin a REACH | the telegraph | harm (predicate) | cooldown | arrives as STALK |
| SUBSIDING | n/a | → RETREAT | resolves or fizzles | harm only if resolving | stays | arrives as RETREAT |

A TELL abort moves the phase from TELL to CALM; the record in STALK returns to LURK with no strike and no mark burst (HE-12). A record in RETREAT during a second TELL goes RETREAT → LURK → STALK on the new `tell_epoch`; it never skips LURK. Predicate check points (each is an acceptance criterion): REACH begin (HE-14 c), STRIKE entry (HE-15), every M17 RUPTURE or GOUGE call (HE-16, HE-29). All three read `exogenous_permitted` at their own tick. CHILL is a non-harm footprint, permitted in any phase, bounded by HE-F4 and HE-31 (C-HE-28).

#### Transition guards (ordered; the first match wins within a run)

1. **Restore-first.** A run that follows a restore re-checks the predicate for any record in REACH (HE-41) before any other guard.
2. **Dormancy.** The body is not loaded → no step (HE-21).
3. **Layout change.** `layout.state` changed since the last run → invalidate the path cache and re-derive `body_id` from `body_of(section_id)` (HE-22).
4. **Phase edge.** A new `tell_epoch` → LURK → STALK or TRANSIT; the phase returned to CALM from TELL → STALK → LURK; SUBSIDING → STALK → RETREAT.
5. **Cycle guards.** In BREACH: STALK → REACH (HE-14), REACH → STRIKE / fizzle, STRIKE → RETREAT, RETREAT → STALK.
6. **Return.** `RETURN_DELAY_TICKS` after the end of SUBSIDING or a TELL abort and not in the home body → TRANSIT.

**Epoch counters.** `strikes_epoch`, `gouges_epoch`, `ruptures_epoch`, `chill_epoch_dj` and `chill_deposits_epoch` reset at TELL entry (a new `tell_epoch`); caps therefore bound each told epoch, and CALM activity after a breach counts against that epoch's remaining allowance until the next TELL.

#### Published fields by state (`horror.view`)

| State | Published beyond the always-present `entity_id`, `type_id`, `state`, `body_id`, `section_id`, `pos_px` |
|---|---|
| LURK, STALK, RETREAT | `state_enter_T`; the disturbance list; diagnostic counters |
| REACH | `locked_pt`, `reach_end_T` (the rig and Audio read them; the player reads the limb) |
| STRIKE | `strike_T`, the seats hit, the hull segments gouged or ruptured, the `tell_epoch` it ran in (TellAudit) |
| TRANSIT | `transit_until_T`, the pending ingress segment once chosen; no `pos_px` |

#### Persisted timers and counters (all in `horror.state`; see HE-41)

`state_enter_T`, `reach_end_T`, `cooldown_until_T`, `transit_until_T`, `last_seen_tell_epoch`, the `"horror.tell"` counter, the per-window footprint count, the per-epoch counts, the mark ring with each mark's `life_left_ticks`, the disturbance list with `last_end_T`, and `lurk_dwell_index`. A restore reconstructs every state from these, never from the previous process's memory (G4 save-equivalence).

#### Cycle timing at the placeholders

Minimum interval between two strike resolutions for a seat that has not moved is `STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS + UNFOLD_LO` = 395 ticks (6.6 s) when the horror is already in reach range. `UNFOLD_TICKS` is `5 × rng_int(19, 36)`: 18 equally likely values from 95 to 180 with mean 137.5, so the mean interval is 437.5 ticks (7.3 s) and the longest is 480 ticks (8.0 s). That is a lower bound: after a strike the horror retreats (96 ticks out at `V_CREEP_MPX`, inside the cooldown), turns at `cooldown_until_T` and walks back at `V_HUNT_MPX` (64 ticks, 65 on the run grid), so the realised period of a seat that does not move is `UNFOLD_TICKS + STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS + 65` = 460 to 545 ticks (7.7 to 9.1 s), mean 502.5 (registered formula HE-F15). The time to a third strike for a seat that never moves, and the share of minimum-length breaches that contain a third REACH start (none), are derived in HE-F16 and are not carried here.

### Interactions with Other Systems

Reading guide. "Neighbour's own row" quotes the dependency GDD's text about Horror Entities verbatim (columns of a quoted table row are separated by ` ‖ `), so that bidirectional consistency can be read in one place. "Path" is the kernel's CR-9 path: (a) a published unconditional mutator called from the caller's own hook, (b) a `SYSTEM`-origin command processed at T+1 when the target may reject. **Horror uses path (a) only and submits no `SYSTEM` command at MVP.** "Class A / Class B" is the mutator class (Class B clamps, never rejects). "Status" is the neighbour's state at authoring time. Rows for undesigned neighbours list **supplies and assumptions only** and are `[PROVISIONAL]`, each registered with the owner named.

#### Upstream — designed

| Neighbour (status) | Neighbour's own row (verbatim) | Horror reads (in) | Horror writes or publishes (out) | Path, class, names fixed there | Consistency and owed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Command & Simulation Layer** (designed) | KRN:201 row 9: "Entity block (`SIM`); hook `after` Attention and Emitter exposure; footprints via the Graph's mutator; light disturbance exposed as state Lighting reads ‖ Tick base (tell windows in ticks); seeded RNG stream helper ‖ Horror ‖ `SYSTEM`-origin commands where a target may reject". KRN:687 Dependencies: "Horror Entities ‖ Hard ‖ Ordered step, RNG streams, `SYSTEM` commands ‖ Entity block, footprint mutator calls ‖ Yes" | tick `T`; `rng_derive(world_seed, "horror.tell", counter)`; `read_view`s; `layout_hash` via Layout | block `horror.state`; hook `horror.step`; `horror.view` | CR-3 hook tuple (HE-3); CR-8 block registry (HE-41); CR-9 path (a) mutators (HE-28); D.10 `rng_derive` (HE-42); D.12 `state_hash`; CR-12 `PERCEPTION_OUT` isolation; H5, H9, H10 | Agrees. The row's "`SYSTEM`-origin commands where a target may reject" is satisfied by *none*: no target of Horror's can reject. The `after` list is exactly the ruled `[attention.step, emitter.place]` (C-HE-20 closed: `body.integrate` precedes `horror.step` through `emitter.place`). The example stream `"horror.tell"` with `rng_int(600, 1800)` (KRN D.10) is an illustration, not Horror's REACH range (HE-9); a refresh of that example is owed to the kernel (Attention's C8 already routes it). |
| **Life-Support Graph** (designed) | LSG:268: "Horror Entities ‖ M17 `deposit_trace` CHILL/BURN/GOUGE/RUPTURE/STRESS (path (a), capped) ‖ `failures()` for route choice, `compartment()` for cold rooms ‖ Graph publishes; Horror calls ‖ Horror hooks any divisor; writes queued to phase 0 ‖ provisional". LSG:863: "Horror Entities ‖ `deposit_trace` footprint channel (CHILL, BURN, GOUGE, RUPTURE; per-step budget); exogenous causes are exempt from the fairness gate ‖ nothing ‖ hook path (a) ‖ Yes" | `compartment(cid)` (the room's `T` for the CHILL guard), `compartment_at(room_ref)`; hull segment ids (read owed, C-HE-11); `failures()` is advisory route choice and unused at MVP | M17 `deposit_trace(kind, subject_id, magnitude)` with kind CHILL, GOUGE (hull only), RUPTURE (hull only); never STRESS, BURN or M18 | M17 Class B, path (a); `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` 16 and `STRESS_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` 8 (CITED); `RUPTURE_HP`, `BREACH_HP`, `FOOTPRINT_MAX_Q`, `FOOTPRINT_MAX_GOUGE_BP`, `STRESS_HOLD_STEPS`, `C_TILE` cited with the registry value and LSG lines (registered 2026-10-27; C-HE-7 closed) | Agrees on kinds and path. Horror narrows the kinds (no BURN, no STRESS) and restricts GOUGE to hull (FT-31).
