### Group T: the tell window and permission (run first; BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-1 (auto) REACH begin gate.** GIVEN a replay fixture in which HE-14 conditions (b) to (e) hold and the phase is forced to each of CALM, TELL, BREACH and SUBSIDING, and a second fixture with the target seat's `actor_section` outside `locus_body`, WHEN `horror.step` runs for 600 ticks in each, THEN the REACH-begin count is 0 in CALM, TELL and SUBSIDING and 0 for the out-of-body seat, and a REACH begins on the first Horror run after the phase becomes BREACH with the seat inside `locus_body`. The Horror-side gate withholds the act; no downstream rejection is relied on. *Covers HE-8 check point (1), HE-14.*
- **AC-HE-2 (auto) STRIKE entry gate.** GIVEN a REACH whose `reach_end_T` run falls in BREACH with the predicate true, in SUBSIDING with the seat's section in `locus_body`, in SUBSIDING after a pod-hop removed the seat from the body, and in CALM after a forced phase reset, WHEN the run executes, THEN `apply_suit_damage` is called once per hit seat in the first two cases and zero times in the last two (which fizzle). *Covers HE-8 check point (2), HE-15, E-HE-10, E-HE-11.*
- **AC-HE-3 (auto) Every hull deposit gate.** GIVEN forced M17 RUPTURE and GOUGE attempts in CALM, TELL, BREACH and SUBSIDING, and one aimed at a hull segment in a body other than `locus_body`, WHEN each is attempted, THEN the call count is 0 in CALM, TELL and for the non-locus segment, and a call is issued in BREACH and SUBSIDING only when `exogenous_permitted(section of the segment)` holds at the call tick. *Covers HE-8 check point (3), HE-16.*
- **AC-HE-4 (auto, watch-it-fail) The audit can fail.** GIVEN the registered formula `tell_window_contract` (for every harm event `e` an epoch `k` with `tell_start_k + tell_len_k <= t_e < subside_end_k`, `tell_len_k >= TELL_MIN_TICKS` 600 and `section_e` in `locus_body_k`, the locus body in force at `t_e`, replaced only by an AT-14 LOCUS_FALLBACK) run as `TellAudit` over a replay of 10 told breaches, WHEN the replay completes, THEN the violation count is 0; AND GIVEN a test double that injects one RUPTURE past the Horror-side gate at `tell_start_k + 599`, THEN the audit records exactly one violation. The assertion quotes the formula, not AT:373's prose (C-HE-28). *Covers HE-8, HE-10.*
- **AC-HE-5 (auto) Crew-body scope.** GIVEN seat A whose Body `actor_section` is in `locus_body` and lit, seat B whose `actor_section` is outside it, and a stub in which Body's `actor_section` and the Graph's `section_of` disagree for seat A, WHEN a BREACH REACH resolves with both seats inside the disc geometry, THEN `apply_suit_damage` is called for seat A only, and the predicate was evaluated on `actor_section`. *Covers HE-8, HE-15, E-HE-55.*
- **AC-HE-6 (lint + review) One harm gate.** GIVEN the Horror source, WHEN every call site of `deposit_trace` with kind RUPTURE or GOUGE and every call site of `apply_suit_damage` is enumerated, THEN each lies inside one harm-gate function that reads `exogenous_permitted` at the call tick (a dataflow property, so a reviewer confirms what the lint finds), none is guarded by the Graph's `fairness_gate`, and no call site names STRESS, BURN or M18. *Covers HE-8, HE-9, HE-28.* -> ADR candidate: the harm-gate function.
- **AC-HE-7 (auto) CHILL is not a harm event.** GIVEN a LURK in CALM and a STALK in TELL that each make CHILL deposits, WHEN the audit of AC-HE-4 runs, THEN it flags none of them and `horror_chill_withheld_cold` and `_cap` stay 0 in nominal play (a fixture with no coolant fitting within `FITTING_CLEARANCE_PX`; `_fitting` is expected to increment at a fitting, AC-HE-61). *Covers HE-8, HE-27, HE-F4, C-HE-28.* **The degrade path** (CHILL withheld outside BREACH and SUBSIDING, rime mark ring-only) is a contingency if the Attention owner rules AT:373's prose literal; no knob or rule implements it, so its criterion reads **NOT ASSESSED** (C-HE-28, Gaps).
- **AC-HE-8 (auto) Fizzle.** GIVEN a REACH in flight, WHEN (i) the phase becomes SUBSIDING with the predicate true, THEN it resolves and then RETREATs; (ii) `SUBSIDE_CANCELS_REACH` is 1 and the phase becomes SUBSIDING, THEN it fizzles; (iii) the seat pod-hops or the phase returns to CALM, THEN it fizzles. In every fizzle there is no `apply_suit_damage`, no hairlines, no GOUGE and no RUPTURE; the touch-down smear stays in the ring; `cooldown_until_T` is unchanged; the `"horror.tell"` counter stays incremented. *Covers HE-8, HE-17, E-HE-10, E-HE-11.*
- **AC-HE-9 (auto) TELL abort.** GIVEN a STALK in a TELL that ends in an abort, WHEN the phase returns to CALM, THEN the record goes STALK to LURK with no strike and the marks inserted on that run number at most `MARKS_PER_RUN_MAX` 2 (no burst). *Covers HE-12.*
- **AC-HE-10 (auto + lint) Hook identity.** GIVEN the hook registry, WHEN `horror.step` is read, THEN the divisor is 5, the offset is 0 and `after` is exactly `[attention.step, emitter.place]`; a topological-order test shows `body.integrate` precedes `horror.step` on every Horror tick through `emitter.place`; a hook id unknown at boot (for example `horror.tick`) fails boot (negative fixture, expected FAIL). *Covers HE-3, HE-4.*
- **AC-HE-11 (auto) Reading between Attention's runs.** GIVEN the phase changes to BREACH on a tick that is not a Horror run, WHEN the next Horror run executes, THEN Horror acts on it no more than 4 ticks later and no harm call precedes `tell_start + tell_len` (the strike never precedes the end of Attention's tell). *Covers HE-4, E-HE-17.*
- **AC-HE-12 (auto, BLOCKING; smoke) A clean-ship breach has teeth.** GIVEN a clean ship (no damage), one lit stationary seat in the locus body, `tell_len` drawn at 600 and at 1800, `UNFOLD_TICKS` at its two extremes, and the horror resident in the locus body, WHEN a TELL then BREACH runs with TELL entry pinned to a tick with `T mod 30 = 0` (so the BREACH entry at `+600` or `+1800` is also a Horror run), THEN the first `apply_suit_damage` lands no earlier than 760 ticks and no later than 2045 ticks after TELL entry (`TELL_MIN_TICKS` 600 + `t_arrive` 65 + `UNFOLD_LO` 95; `TELL_MAX_TICKS` 1800 + 65 + `UNFOLD_HI` 180), on the seat that did not move. *Covers AT-11 teeth, HE-1, HE-14, HE-F13.*
- **AC-HE-13 (lint) Boundaries.** GIVEN the Horror source and data, WHEN scanned, THEN there is no call to `push_stimulus`, `set_beacon` or `set_opening`; no write to any Attention field; no write to a seat position or `loco_state`; no new Graph footprint kind; no sound, mix or cue name; no HUD node; no stimulus tracker, accumulator or field named `threat`, `aggro`, `alert`, `awareness`, `suspicion` or `hate`; and every transition and harm gate reads only the listed `attention.view` fields (`phase`, `tell_epoch`, `tell_outcome`, `locus_section_id`, `exogenous_permitted`, `breach_enter_T`, `phase_enter_T`). *Covers HE-6, HE-7, HE-36.*
- **AC-HE-14 (auto) Non-lethal outside BREACH.** GIVEN a 36000-tick CALM fixture with a seat that walks toward the horror at `V_WALK`, WHEN the run completes, THEN no RUPTURE, GOUGE or `apply_suit_damage` was issued; every state change out of LURK was caused by `attention.view.phase`; and no step taken by the horror ended closer to any living seat than `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` 192 unless the distance before the step was already smaller, in which case the step did not reduce it. *Covers HE-11, E-HE-7. (orchestrator ruling: the standoff is a constraint on the horror's own motion, C3; a seat may close on a horror that steps away at 1 px per tick.)*
- **AC-HE-15 (auto) Walk-through.** GIVEN a seat that walks onto the horror in CALM, in TELL and between REACHes, WHEN the two overlap, THEN there is no collision, no suit damage, no state change other than the horror stepping away (or holding still when cornered), and no mark burst. *Covers HE-2, HE-11, E-HE-25.*
- **AC-HE-16 (auto) STALK in a TELL.** GIVEN a TELL fixture, WHEN the horror STALKs, THEN its per-tick step matches HE-F6 at `V_CREEP_MPX` 1000 (always 1 px), no step ends within `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` of a living seat, and it issues no RUPTURE, GOUGE or `apply_suit_damage`. *Covers HE-12, HE-F6.*

### Group B: the behaviour cycle (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-17 (auto) LURK dwell and waypoints.** GIVEN a LURK in CALM, WHEN 3 dwells complete, THEN it dwells `LURK_DWELL_TICKS` 900 at each waypoint; the next waypoint is the lowest `hash(entity_id, dwell_index, HE_HASH_SEED)` among waypoints in loaded sections of its body at least `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` from every living seat; the same seed gives the same sequence; and with no legal waypoint it holds still and lays no mark. *Covers HE-11, E-HE-7.*
- **AC-HE-18 (auto) TELL entry and phase edges.** GIVEN a new `tell_epoch` differing from `last_seen_tell_epoch`, WHEN the next run executes, THEN LURK goes to STALK when the locus body is its body and to TRANSIT otherwise; in BREACH the approach closes to `REACH_RANGE_PX` 96 of the target point (not only to the standoff); SUBSIDING sends STALK to RETREAT. *Covers HE-12.*
- **AC-HE-19 (auto) Target is the lit seat.** GIVEN seat A with the lamp on (`lux_pm` 1000) and seat B dark (0), WHEN a run executes, THEN the target is A. *Covers HE-13, HE-F8.*
- **AC-HE-20 (auto) Going dark.** GIVEN seat A switches its lamp off with no other lit seat, WHEN the next run executes, THEN the target is ANCHOR and nothing remembers A. *Covers HE-13, HE-F8.*
- **AC-HE-21 (auto) The target is not stored.** GIVEN a snapshot and restore in mid-STALK, WHEN the next run executes, THEN it recomputes the same target from `lux` and the snapshot carries no target field and no path cache. *Covers HE-13, HE-41.*
- **AC-HE-22 (auto) Light does nothing to the horror.** GIVEN identical geometry lit and unlit, WHEN 600 ticks run, THEN the horror's movement is identical (no repel, stun, slow or harm by light). *Covers HE-13, EM:89.*
- **AC-HE-23 (auto) Lock.** GIVEN a lit seat within `REACH_RANGE_PX` in BREACH, WHEN a REACH begins, THEN P equals the seat's foot point at that run and never changes while the seat moves away; the body's position is unchanged from `reach_start_T` through the STRIKE hold. *Covers HE-14.*
- **AC-HE-24 (auto) The draw.** GIVEN a REACH begins with `"horror.tell"` counter 7 and a stubbed `rng_int(19, 36)` of 22, WHEN the block is written, THEN `UNFOLD_TICKS` is 110, a multiple of 5 within [`UNFOLD_LO` 95, `UNFOLD_HI` 180]; `reach_end_T = reach_start_T + 110` is a multiple of 5; the counter reads 8 in the same block write; and the only random source is `rng_derive(world_seed, "horror.tell", counter)`. *Covers HE-14, HE-F5, HE-42.*
- **AC-HE-25 (auto) The strike publishes its facts on the run of the state change.** GIVEN a REACH that resolves, WHEN `horror.view` is read at the end of that run, THEN `locked_pt` and `reach_end_T` were published from the run that began the REACH (no one-run lag), `strike_T` and the seats hit are published on the STRIKE entry run, and a touch-down SMEAR exists at P from the REACH-begin run. *Covers HE-14, HE-15, HE-37.*
- **AC-HE-26 (auto) No marker.** GIVEN a REACH in flight, WHEN the presenter and the mark ring are inspected, THEN the only thing at P is the limb tip and the touch-down SMEAR (a true ring entry); no ground decal, ring, arc, disc, radius or victim-side lock mark exists. *Covers HE-14, CP-1.*
- **AC-HE-27 (auto) Strike geometry boundary.** GIVEN `R_SNAP_PX` 72, P = (400, 300) and seats at dx = 71, 72 and 73 px (axis-aligned) in the same room, one seat in the next room at 60 px across a wall, and one DOWNED seat at 10 px, WHEN the strike resolves, THEN only the 71 px seat is hit (`71² = 5041 < 5184`; 72 gives `5184 < 5184` false); exactly one `apply_suit_damage(player_id, STRIKE_BP, source_ref)` call is made per hit seat in ascending player id; `source_ref` carries the EXOGENOUS class; the comparison uses integers only. *Covers HE-15, HE-F2, E-HE-1, E-HE-35.*
- **AC-HE-28 (auto, integration with Suit) The three-strike ladder.** GIVEN integrity 10000 and `STRIKE_BP` 3200, WHEN three strikes land, THEN integrity reads 6800 (STRAINED), 3600 (CRITICAL: stagger, walk-only) and 400 (FAILED, then DOWNED); a fourth strike is never issued on the DOWNED seat; a seat struck at 3600 ends at 400 and FAILED; and with no living non-DOWNED seat remaining, the target becomes ANCHOR: after the cooldown the record STALKs to the anchor and its REACHes call no `apply_suit_damage` (HE-13, HE-16 (3)); it is never in LURK while the phase is BREACH. GIVEN `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1, THEN the rung is set at the next `suit.vitals` run (at most 6 ticks after the hit) with no hold (SE-23). *Covers HE-15, HE-18, HE-F3, E-HE-2.*
- **AC-HE-29 (auto) The strike hold.** GIVEN a STRIKE, WHEN it resolves, THEN the state stays STRIKE for `STRIKE_HOLD_RUNS` 2 runs (10 ticks), begins on the first run with `T >= reach_end_T`, and a seat on a door sill belongs to the sill's room. *Covers HE-15.*
- **AC-HE-30 (auto) GOUGE subject and contact.** GIVEN a strike with P within `HULL_CONTACT_PX` 24 of a hull-segment face, WHEN it resolves, THEN a GOUGE is deposited on that segment and radial hairlines are laid at the contact point; a duct, cable, pipe or door is never a subject (lint `GOUGE_NON_HULL`). *Covers HE-16, FT-31.* *(provisional — Layout/Graph hull-segment lookup owed, C-HE-11.)*
- **AC-HE-31 (auto) Epoch caps.** GIVEN four consecutive strikes at one hull face in one `tell_epoch`, WHEN each resolves, THEN at most `GOUGES_PER_EPOCH_MAX` 3 GOUGE and `RUPTURES_PER_EPOCH_MAX` 1 RUPTURE are deposited and all five epoch counters reset at the next TELL entry. *Covers HE-16, HE-F17.* *(provisional — C-HE-11.)*
- **AC-HE-32 (auto) The ladder gate.** GIVEN strike one on a fresh segment at T = 1075, strike two on the same segment at T = 1550 and strike three on it later, WHEN each resolves, THEN strike one deposits GOUGE, strike two deposits RUPTURE (`1550 >= 1075 + 300`, gouged, `ruptures_epoch 0 < 1`) and strike three deposits GOUGE; one hull deposit kind per STRIKE; a RUPTURE never occurs on an un-gouged segment or earlier than `STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS` after the GOUGE. *Covers HE-16, HE-F17, E-HE-12.* *(provisional — C-HE-11.)*
- **AC-HE-33 (auto) Anchor REACH.** GIVEN no lit seat, WHEN the anchor REACH resolves, THEN the anchor is the lowest-id anchor of the locus section's outermost compartment within `HULL_CONTACT_PX` of a hull face; it lays hairlines and, with `ANCHOR_REACH_GOUGES` 1, one GOUGE; it never issues RUPTURE or `apply_suit_damage`; with no such anchor it lays hairlines only. *Covers HE-16 (3), E-HE-21.* *(provisional — C-HE-11.)*
- **AC-HE-34 (auto) A dark seat beside the anchor is not harmed.** GIVEN a dark seat parked within `R_SNAP_PX` of the anchor at an anchor REACH's resolution, WHEN it resolves, THEN `apply_suit_damage` is not called and the hull takes the GOUGE. *Covers E-HE-22.*
- **AC-HE-35 (auto) Cooldown.** GIVEN a strike at T, WHEN the next REACH is attempted, THEN none begins before `T + STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS` 300; GIVEN a fizzle, THEN the next REACH may begin immediately; GIVEN `STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS` 30 (at `STAGGER_TICKS`), THEN the data load fails (negative fixture, expected FAIL). *Covers HE-17, HE-F15.*
- **AC-HE-36 (auto) Never what is already down.** GIVEN a seat DOWNED or DEAD at candidate selection, or DOWNED mid-REACH by another cause (a teammate's friendly harm), or IN_POD, WHEN the horror acts, THEN it is not a candidate and is not hit, and the REACH completes harmlessly to RETREAT; a STAGGERED seat remains a candidate. *Covers HE-13, HE-18, E-HE-18, E-HE-53.*
- **AC-HE-37 (auto + lint) Door leaf.** GIVEN a closed door leaf between the horror and P, WHEN the strike resolves, THEN no seat on the far side is hit, no `probe()` or physics query occurred in the run, and the segment test used the room-graph sill. *Covers HE-19, E-HE-19.*
- **AC-HE-38 (auto) Late lamp-off and layout change at resolution.** GIVEN the lamp is switched off after P is locked, THEN the strike still resolves at P and hits every living seat in the disc and only the next run's target changes; GIVEN a weld or cut on the resolving tick, THEN P is unchanged, the same-room and closed-leaf tests are re-evaluated against the current layout, and a layout change can only spare a seat. *Covers HE-19, E-HE-14, E-HE-23.* *(provisional — Modular Ship Assembly designed in this run.)*
- **AC-HE-39 (auto) REACH from a cleared cooldown.** GIVEN a lit seat already within `REACH_RANGE_PX` at the run the cooldown clears, WHEN that run executes, THEN the REACH begins and its telegraph is at least `UNFOLD_LO`. *Covers E-HE-6.*
- **AC-HE-40 (auto) RETREAT.** GIVEN a STRIKE, WHEN RETREAT runs, THEN it moves at `V_CREEP_MPX` to the nearest waypoint at least `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` from every living seat; it goes to STALK when `T >= cooldown_until_T` in BREACH, to LURK in CALM or TELL when not returning; and a second TELL during RETREAT goes RETREAT, LURK, STALK, never skipping LURK, resetting `strikes_epoch`, `gouges_epoch`, `ruptures_epoch`, `chill_epoch_dj` and `chill_deposits_epoch`. *Covers HE-17, E-HE-16.*

### Group P: presence, movement and counterplay (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-41 (auto) Ingress choice.** GIVEN a TELL whose `locus_body` is not the horror's body and seats at known `lux_pm` values, WHEN ingress completes, THEN the arrival hull segment lies in a room one hop from the room of the highest-`lux_pm` seat (none lit: the locus section's outermost compartment), in a room holding no living seat and at least `LURK_STANDOFF_PX` from every living seat; ties lowest hop, then lowest room id, then lowest hull-segment id. *Covers HE-20, HE-F9.*
- **AC-HE-42 (auto) Ingress timing.** GIVEN TELL entry at T = 1200 with tell_len 900, WHEN ingress runs, THEN `transit_until_T` is 1500, the ingress mark (smear and hairlines) is laid at 1440 (`INGRESS_MARK_LEAD_TICKS` 60 before the body), the body registers at the run at 1500 and is in STALK, BREACH begins at 2100 and 600 ticks of stalk were published (at least `TELL_MIN_TICKS − INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS` = 300); `INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS` 400 fails the relation (`400 > 600 − 240 = 360`, negative fixture, expected FAIL); `D_entry_max` is 492 px. *Covers HE-20, HE-F9.*
- **AC-HE-43 (auto) Registration order.** GIVEN an ingress, WHEN it runs, THEN E7 (egress mark at the old site) precedes E6 on arrival, E8 runs once per run, `S_h` stays 1, and a seat entering the chosen room during the mark-lead window forces a re-choice by the same rule while the first hairline stays. *Covers HE-20, HE-34, E-HE-34.*
- **AC-HE-44 (auto, negative fixture, expected FAIL) Cry-wolf.** GIVEN no legal ingress entry, WHEN the record stays in TRANSIT, THEN `horror_ingress_pending` is raised; a fixture that enters BREACH with ingress pending fails the build. *Covers HE-20, E-HE-33.*
- **AC-HE-45 (auto) TRANSIT.** GIVEN a record in TRANSIT, WHEN runs execute, THEN it is unregistered, publishes no `pos_px`, lays no marks, harms nothing, and its arrival state follows the phase view (CALM LURK, TELL and BREACH STALK, SUBSIDING RETREAT); an abort on the arrival run arrives as LURK. *Covers HE-20, E-HE-15.*
- **AC-HE-46 (auto) Return.** GIVEN `RETURN_HOME` 1, WHEN SUBSIDING ends or a TELL aborts, THEN after `RETURN_DELAY_TICKS` 600 the record returns to its home body by the HE-20 rule; GIVEN `RETURN_HOME` 0, THEN it remains and LURKs in the body of the last breach. *Covers HE-20.*
- **AC-HE-47 (auto) Dormancy.** GIVEN a record whose body unloads, WHEN 10000 ticks pass and the body reloads, THEN the record is byte-identical to its state at unload, no mark aged and no disturbance ran; a TRANSIT record whose destination unloads waits. *Covers HE-21, E-HE-43.*
- **AC-HE-48 (auto) Weld and cut.** GIVEN a weld mid-path and a cut removing the record's section, WHEN the next run executes, THEN the path cache is recomputed, `body_id` is re-derived from `body_of`, a record with no section goes TRANSIT with an egress mark, and `locus_body` is unchanged by the weld (only an AT-14 fallback, recorded as LOCUS_FALLBACK, replaces it mid-epoch). *Covers HE-22, E-HE-29, E-HE-30.* *(provisional — Modular Ship Assembly designed in this run.)*
- **AC-HE-49 (auto) Speed cadence.** GIVEN `V_HUNT_MPX` 1500 and `acc` 0, WHEN a run executes, THEN `v_eff` per tick is 1, 2, 1, 2, 1 (7 px) and the next run 2, 1, 2, 1, 2 (8 px); at `V_CREEP_MPX` 1000 it is always 1 (5 px); a walking seat and a CRITICAL walk-only seat each outpace the hunting horror; a carrier (`V_CARRY` 1) does not (intended, C-HE-8). The data load refuses any `V_HUNT_MPX >= V_WALK × 1000` (2000) and any `V_CREEP_MPX` below 1000. *Covers HE-24, HE-F6.*
- **AC-HE-50 (auto) Octant stepping.** GIVEN a direction to the next waypoint, WHEN a run executes, THEN one octant is chosen (the integer argmax of the eight unit vectors, ties lowest octant), the horror takes five steps with it, positions and residue persist, and `pos_prev` is the position at run start. *Covers HE-23.*
- **AC-HE-51 (auto) Pathing.** GIVEN a 14-room body, WHEN the path is computed on a cache miss, THEN expansions are at most `PATH_EXPANSIONS_MAX` (14 on the slice, bound 64); a hit costs 0; a closed leaf changes the cache key; a NONE or UNKNOWN Layout read holds the waypoint for at most `HOLD_MAX_RUNS` 12 then LURKs and is never read as unlit or as open; zero `probe()` calls; after `BLOCKED_RUNS_MAX` 6 no-movement runs the cached hop is discarded and re-pathed exactly once. *Covers HE-25, HE-F10, E-HE-31, E-HE-32.*
- **AC-HE-52 (auto) A sealed target.** GIVEN the highest-`lux_pm` seat sealed behind a closed leaf, WHEN the horror acts, THEN it stops in the last reachable room at the leaf, begins no REACH, does not switch to a dimmer reachable seat and `apply_suit_damage` count is 0. *Covers E-HE-20.*
- **AC-HE-53 (auto) Counterplay, one fixture each.** GIVEN each of the six MVP responses, WHEN executed, THEN the outcome holds. (a) *Retreat:* a seat walking away at `V_WALK` from a hunting horror gains 500 milli-px per tick and is never inside `R_SNAP_PX` at `reach_end_T`. (b) *Close a door:* no hit through the leaf and no `set_opening` call from Horror. (c) *Go dark:* the next run's target is ANCHOR and `apply_suit_damage` count is 0 that breach. (d) *Pod-hop:* the departed seat leaves `locus_body`, `exogenous_permitted(actor_section(seat))` fails and the in-flight REACH on it fizzles; a home-ship breach, or a co-op seat that is not the last recorded occupant, does not by itself end the breach (AT-12 (iii)). (e) *Drop cargo:* a carrier whose speed becomes `V_WALK` inside 54 ticks clears at 36 ticks; one that does not is caught except on the 11 of 18 draws with `UNFOLD_TICKS >= 130`. (f) *Glowstick work-light:* a Chemlight pool is never a target; a seat standing in it is lit and is a candidate. *Covers HE-26, E-HE-19, E-HE-24, E-HE-27.*
- **AC-HE-54 (auto + review) Dead-end authoring defect.** GIVEN a seat in a dead-end room with a floor span below 144 px and a single exit, WHEN a REACH resolves at P in that room, THEN the seat is struck without a legal walk-out and the case is recorded as an authoring defect, not a Horror rule; the lint is an owed row to Derelict Authoring. *Covers E-HE-26.* *(provisional — Derelict Authoring lint owed.)*

### Group F: footprints and marks (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-55 (auto; smoke) Window budget and saturation.** GIVEN window 5 holds the Horror runs at T = 30 and T = 35, with the run at 30 issuing GOUGE and CHILL (2) and the run at 35 issuing CHILL (1), WHEN windows 0 to 5 execute, THEN `n_5` is 3 and windows 1 to 4 (one run each at T = 10, 15, 20, 25) are within 8; GIVEN a stub requesting 20 deposits in window 5 while Attention also writes 8 STRESS, THEN Horror is granted 4 plus 4, `horror_footprints_withheld` is 12 and the Graph sees 16 and drops none; nominal play of a full breach leaves the counter at 0; `MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` 5 fails the data load (`2 × 5 > 8`, negative fixture, expected FAIL). *Covers HE-29, HE-F1, E-HE-45.*
- **AC-HE-56 (auto) Priority under the cap.** GIVEN a window already at 6 accepted calls and a run requesting RUPTURE, GOUGE and CHILL, WHEN it executes, THEN RUPTURE and GOUGE are issued (8), CHILL is withheld, its rime mark is still laid and `horror_footprints_withheld` increments by 1. *Covers E-HE-13.*
- **AC-HE-57 (auto) Mark production.** GIVEN a creep of 480 px and one REACH, WHEN the ring is read, THEN a SMEAR was laid per `MARK_SPACING_PX` 96 of travel plus one touch-down SMEAR per REACH start, a RIME per CHILL deposit (per `CHILL_SPACING_PX` 384 and at each LURK dwell start), HAIRLINES at each STRIKE, GOUGE, RUPTURE, anchor REACH, ingress and egress, one STRUCK per STRIKE on which `apply_suit_damage` was called and none on a miss (AC-HE-121); a stationary horror lays nothing. *Covers HE-27, HE-30.*
- **AC-HE-58 (auto) Per-run mark cap and ids.** GIVEN a run that wants three inserts, WHEN it executes, THEN the first `MARKS_PER_RUN_MAX` 2 are inserted in the order STRUCK, HAIRLINES, RIME, SMEAR, the rest are counted `horror_marks_withheld`, and `mark_id` is never reused. *Covers HE-30, HE-F7, E-HE-9.*
- **AC-HE-59 (auto) Ring eviction.** GIVEN a full ring (24) holding hairline marks 4, 5, 6, five rime marks and sixteen smears, WHEN smear id 31 is inserted, THEN the oldest smear (id 1) is evicted and no hairline; GIVEN a ring with no smear, THEN the lowest `mark_id` of any kind is evicted. *Covers HE-30, HE-F7, E-HE-8.*
- **AC-HE-60 (auto) Lifetime and stages.** GIVEN marks in a loaded section, WHEN runs execute, THEN `life_left` falls by 5 per run only for loaded sections; the stage is `min(2, floor(3 × age / MARK_LIFE_TICKS))` (a mark with `life_left` 12000 is stage 1; at 5999 stage 2); every mutation declares explicit dirty keys (a missing key fails the save-equivalence test, AC-HE-76). *Covers HE-30, HE-F7, E-HE-46.*
- **AC-HE-61 (lint + review) Rime and mark honesty.** GIVEN every Horror mark asset swatch and the generating code, WHEN scanned, THEN no flesh (hue 5-30, S 20-60 %, V 45-85 %), no violet (hue 270-330, S above 25 %), no Alarm Red, Chemlight or Tungsten; rime is hue-less; branch angles are a pure function of the mark's `seed` and two marks with different seeds do not share an identical angle set; no rime within `FITTING_CLEARANCE_PX` 48 of a coolant fitting (the deposit and the mark are both withheld there, `horror_chill_withheld_fitting` increments). The rendered-pixel half (L\* 70 to 78 on the lit floor) is in AC-HE-93. *Covers HE-31.* *The art-director's proposal that no two adjacent branch spacings lie within 5 degrees is not in HE-31; it is carried as an Open Question (C2) and is not asserted here.*
- **AC-HE-62 (auto) CHILL bound.** GIVEN `CHILL_DEPOSITS_PER_EPOCH_MAX` 24 deposits of `CHILL_STEP_DJ` 3000, WHEN summed, THEN the epoch total is 50 mC per deposit and 1200 mC in a 20-tile room, 2000 mC in a 12-tile room and 3000 mC in an 8-tile room (lint: at `n_tiles_min` 8 the total is at most `CHILL_EPOCH_MAX_MC` 5000); a room at −9000 mC deposits (−9050 ≥ −10000) and a room at −9990 mC withholds (−10040 < −10000, `horror_chill_withheld_cold`) with the rime mark still laid; cumulative Horror CHILL never brings a compartment to `COLD_DMG_ONSET_MC` −15000; `CHILL_STEP_DJ > FOOTPRINT_MAX_Q` fails the load (negative fixture, expected FAIL). *Covers HE-31, HE-F4.*
- **AC-HE-63 (auto) Filter bit-identity.** GIVEN the same replay with and without a Perception Filter stub that hides the body from one seat, WHEN both run, THEN `state_hash` of `horror.state`, every M17 write, every `apply_suit_damage` call, `horror.view.disturbances[]` and the mark ring are bit-identical; the touch-down smear survives a HIDE entry; Sanity-state fringes never alter marks. *Covers HE-32, E-HE-48, E-HE-51.* *(provisional — Perception Filter undesigned; Sanity designed in this run.)*
- **AC-HE-64 (auto) Phantoms leave nothing.** GIVEN a phantom horror injected by a Filter stub and co-occurring with a real one, WHEN the replay runs, THEN `horror.state` gains no record or mark, no M17 call is made for it, nothing is registered with the Emitter, TellAudit records nothing for it, and the real one proceeds on its own clock. *Covers HE-32, E-HE-49.* *(provisional — Perception Filter undesigned.)*
- **AC-HE-121 (auto) The hit mark.** GIVEN two identical replays of one REACH that differ only in whether a seat stands inside `R_SNAP_PX` of P at `reach_end_T`, WHEN each STRIKE entry run completes, THEN the hit replay holds exactly one STRUCK mark at P (one mark even with two seats in the disc) and the miss replay holds none; both hold the touch-down SMEAR laid at REACH start; GIVEN a REACH that fizzles, THEN no STRUCK mark is laid; GIVEN a seat in the disc but a failed predicate, THEN no suit hit and no STRUCK mark; the STRUCK mark survives a restore bit-identically, is not evicted while any SMEAR is resident, and is laid identically with and without a Perception Filter stub (HE-32). *Covers HE-15, HE-27, HE-30, validation target (1).*

### Group L: light and registration (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-65 (auto) Disturbance eligibility.** GIVEN a horror near a mixed set of lamps (FIXTURE, NODE_LANTERN, `fx_cryo_lamp`, BEAM, BEACON, FAULT_GLOW and a Chemlight DEPLOYABLE) and the phase forced through CALM, TELL, BREACH and SUBSIDING for 5000 ticks each, WHEN runs execute, THEN disturbances start in every phase and only on FIXTURE and NODE_LANTERN within `L3_RADIUS_PX` 144 of the horror; never on the EMERGENCY subkind; `strength_pm` is at most `1000 − MOD_FLOOR_PM`; `dur_ticks` is in [`L3_DUR_LO` 18, `L3_DUR_HI` 30]; the gap per lamp is at least `L3_MIN_GAP_TICKS` 120; at most `L3_MAX_ACTIVE` 4 are active; the `last_end_T` map never exceeds `L3_MAX_TRACKED` 16; the disturbance list is persisted; over the first 5 disturbances on one lamp no two share the same (`strength_pm`, `dur_ticks`) pair; Horror never writes an Emitter field or calls Lighting. *Covers HE-35, HE-F11, C-HE-29.*
- **AC-HE-66 (auto) L3 arithmetic.** GIVEN `MOD_FLOOR_PM` 600, WHEN hash h2 = 977, THEN `strength_pm` = 150 + (977 mod 251) = 374; GIVEN `MOD_FLOOR_PM` changed to 800, THEN `S_hi` re-derives to 200 and `L3_STRENGTH_LO_PM` above it fails the load; no result is below `MOD_FLOOR_PM` after Lighting's `modifier_stack`. *Covers HE-F11.*
- **AC-HE-67 (auto, reruns EM AC-24 and AC-LO-16) No occluder, no emission.** GIVEN a lit scene with and without a real horror between an emitter and a subject, WHEN compared, THEN `lux`, `lit`, `lit_by`, the occluder set, the shadow render and `emitters()` are identical. *Covers HE-2.*
- **AC-HE-68 (auto) Registration.** GIVEN boot and ingress, WHEN the record first registers, THEN E6 returns true, E8 runs once per run and E7 at egress or unload; GIVEN a restore, THEN the Emitter registration set equals the pre-save set (EM AC-42), any `post_restore` E6 on an already-registered ref returns false and is not counted as a defect, and `state_hash` is unchanged; `S_h <= HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` 2; `HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX + A_max <= MAX_MOVING_SUBJECTS` (2 + 4 ≤ 16); a stub forcing a first-registration E6 false (negative fixture, expected FAIL) fails the build as a budget defect while the horror still moves and strikes. *Covers HE-34, HE-F12, E-HE-44.*
- **AC-HE-69 (auto + lint) No noise push.** GIVEN a full breach cycle, WHEN `push_stimulus` call counts are read, THEN Horror made zero calls (AC-AT-95 refuses a Horror double as a caller; Horror is not a declared caller and makes zero calls). *Covers HE-36.*

### Group I: information, rules and reward (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-70 (auto) The published fields.** GIVEN each state, WHEN `horror.view` is read, THEN it publishes always `entity_id`, `type_id`, `state`, `body_id`, `section_id`, `pos_px`; LURK, STALK and RETREAT add `state_enter_T`, the disturbance list and the counters; REACH adds `locked_pt` and `reach_end_T`; STRIKE adds `strike_T`, the seats hit, the hull segments gouged or ruptured and the `tell_epoch` it ran in; TRANSIT adds `transit_until_T` and the pending ingress segment and has no `pos_px`; `type_id` and `tell_id` enumerations are stable across a version bump. *Covers HE-37.*
- **AC-HE-71 (review) Audio family disjointness.** GIVEN the Adaptive Audio GDD when it exists, WHEN reviewed, THEN the Horror audio fact names are disjoint from Attention's tell family, the Graph loop sounds and Sanity's hallucination family, phantom horrors use the Filter and Sanity classes and never the Graph witness classes, and Horror never mutes, ducks or filters anything. *Covers HE-37.* *(provisional — Adaptive Audio & Tells undesigned.)*
- **AC-HE-72 (lint + auto) No combat, no reward.** GIVEN the data files and the repository, WHEN scanned, THEN there is no field named `damage`, `accuracy`, `armour`, `dps`, `xp`, `unlock`, `health`, `hp`, `kill`, `loot`, `threat`, `aggro` or `hate`; no hit flash, damage number, health pip, threat bar, proximity ring or shake call from a Horror source; every Interaction verb, iterated by name from the Interaction data, forced at a HORROR subject returns `INVALID_TARGET`; nothing drops, unlocks or awards. *Covers HE-5, HE-38, E-HE-28.*
- **AC-HE-73 (review) Suppressant.** GIVEN `tool_suppressant` and `repel(origin, T)`, WHEN reviewed, THEN no effect ships at MVP; every `repel` criterion (RETREAT for `REPEL_TICKS` 600 within `REPEL_RADIUS_PX` 192, no REACH or STRIKE in the window, never dies, drops nothing, never touches Attention) reads **NOT ASSESSED**, never PASS; C-HE-6 is carried to the creative-director (position confirmed at the CD-GDD-ALIGN gate; gate AC-HE-124); the counterplay set of HE-26 contains no tool. *Covers HE-39, HE-26.*
- **AC-HE-74 (auto + lint) Rig presenter contract.** GIVEN the rig presenter, WHEN driven by a Horror ref, THEN `leak` is 0; a rig with no Horror ref and `leak` 0 is a lint error (negative fixture, expected FAIL); the pose changes only on ticks that are multiples of 5; segment rotation is a multiple of 15 degrees; Horror never writes the camera. *Covers HE-40, HE-F18.* The violet and flesh pixel scan is in AC-HE-93 and the camera-jolt refusal of a Horror-tagged caller is *(provisional — Pipeline jolt API)*.
- **AC-HE-75 (lint) One type row and the data contract.** GIVEN the data and the boot check, WHEN a second type row or `HORROR_MAX_MVP` 2 is configured, THEN the data lint and the boot check refuse it; the behaviour code contains no numeric literal for any row field; every field of the HE-5 row is present. *Covers HE-1, HE-5, E-HE-54, R26.*

### Group S: state, restore and determinism (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-76 (auto) Block conformance.** GIVEN `horror.state`, WHEN conformance tests run, THEN class SIM, `persist` true, `persist_scope` WORLD, scope SHARED, `repl_hint` SHARED_SUMMARY, `schema_version` 1, `supported_versions` [1], `restore_after` `[layout.state, graph.state, emitter.state, attention.state]`; plain data only (no `StringName` keys, no `Vector2i` values); every field of HE-41 is present; the snapshot contains neither the target nor the path cache; `snapshot(restore(snapshot()))` is byte-identical; the ring never exceeds `MAX_MARKS` 24. *Covers HE-41, E-HE-46.*
- **AC-HE-77 (auto) The six restore codes, one crafted save each.** GIVEN `HORROR_RESTORE_SCHEMA` (schema_version 2), `_RANGE` (`acc_mpx` 1000, or `strikes_epoch` above its cap), `_STATE_INCOHERENT` (a TRANSIT record with a registered subject), `_SECTION_UNKNOWN` (a position in a section id Layout does not know), `_MARK_RING` (ring length 25) and `_RNG_COUNTER` (a negative or non-integer `"horror.tell"` counter), WHEN each is restored, THEN it fails hard with the matching code and the Save/Load layer refuses the file; nothing is clamped. *Covers HE-41, E-HE-40, SL-15.* *The `RNG_COUNTER` trigger is not defined in HE-41; this row proposes "negative or non-integer" and asks whether a tighter bound is wanted (C-HE-30).*
- **AC-HE-78 (auto) Restore into REACH.** GIVEN a save taken in REACH with the predicate true, WHEN restored, THEN `reach_end_T`, `locked_pt` and the `"horror.tell"` counter are unchanged (the remaining telegraph is exactly `reach_end_T − T_restore`, never shortened or lengthened, no reroll) and the run that follows matches the uninterrupted run; GIVEN the predicate false at restore, THEN RETREAT with no strike, no hairlines and no GOUGE or RUPTURE. *Covers HE-41, E-HE-36, E-HE-37.*
- **AC-HE-79 (auto) Restore into STRIKE and TRANSIT.** GIVEN saves in STRIKE and in TRANSIT with `transit_until_T` in the past, WHEN restored, THEN no second `apply_suit_damage` or M17 call is made for the STRIKE and the TRANSIT record arrives at the next legal run. *Covers E-HE-38, E-HE-39.*
- **AC-HE-80 (auto) Determinism.** GIVEN two runs with the same `world_seed` and inputs, WHEN 36000 ticks complete, THEN `state_hash` matches every 600 ticks and at every state change; the only random source is `rng_derive(world_seed, "horror.tell", counter)`; mark seeds, LURK waypoints and L3 timing are pure integer hashes; a lint finds no wall clock and no global RNG. *Covers HE-42, E-HE-47.*
- **AC-HE-81 (auto) Save-equivalence at named saves.** GIVEN block-level snapshots (not Save/Load autosaves, since SL-5 defers an autosave in a told breach) taken for each of LURK, STALK, REACH, STRIKE, RETREAT and TRANSIT at state entry, entry plus one run, the midpoint and the last run, WHEN each is restored and run for 1800 ticks, THEN `state_hash`, the mark ring and every footprint equal the uninterrupted run (G4). *Covers HE-41.*
- **AC-HE-82 (auto) A death restarts from the autosave.** GIVEN the player dies at a third strike, WHEN the game restarts from the newest autosave, THEN `horror.state` restores with the WORLD blocks including the `"horror.tell"` counter, so the replay draws the same `UNFOLD_TICKS` sequence; any snapshot taken in CALM has `state` in {LURK, RETREAT, TRANSIT} and never REACH or STRIKE. *Covers HE-10, E-HE-41, E-HE-42.*
- **AC-HE-83 (auto, exhaustive) The phase by state matrix.** GIVEN the 4 phases by 6 states table of States and Transitions, WHEN each of the 24 cells is driven by a fixture, THEN the cell holds (resident, to-LURK, fizzle, cannot begin, hunt, harm on predicate, cooldown, arrives-as); AND for each of the six ordered guards one precedence fixture holds, including restore-first beating a phase edge on the same run and a layout change preceding the phase edge. *Covers the phase view and the transition guards.*

### Group Q: formula criteria (BLOCKING; values inlined)

- **AC-HE-84 (auto) HE-F1.** The worked window example of AC-HE-55 and the priority RUPTURE, GOUGE, CHILL under the cap of AC-HE-56 hold; `2 × MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN <= HE_FOOTPRINT_BUDGET` 8.
- **AC-HE-85 (auto) HE-F2.** Boundary dx 71, 72, 73 as in AC-HE-27; a walker from dx 0 east at 2 px per tick is hit at 35 ticks (`dx = 70`, 4900 < 5184) and clear at 36 (`5184 < 5184` false); a runner is clear at 24 and a carrier at 72; integer comparison only (no float, no `sqrt`).
- **AC-HE-86 (auto, parameter sweep) HE-F3.** For every `STRIKE_BP` in 3001..3749, `k_fail(10000) = 3` and `I_2 >= SUIT_HP_FAIL`; for 3000 and 3750 the data load fails (negative fixtures, expected FAIL); `k_fail(7000) = 2`; `k_fail(SUIT_PATCH_CAP_BP 6000) = 2` (a patched suit: 2800 then 0).
- **AC-HE-87 (auto) HE-F4.** Worked values 50, 1200, 2000 and 3000 mC; thresholds −9050 passes and −10040 fails; as AC-HE-62.
- **AC-HE-88 (auto) HE-F5.** `UNFOLD_LO` 95 passes; 90 fails the bound (negative fixture, expected FAIL); 91 to 94 fail the multiple-of-5 lint; a walker whose walk command is issued at tick 54 is clear at tick 91 and never hit at any of the 18 legal draws; a walker whose command is 5 ticks late (tick 59) is clear at 96 and is hit only on the draw of 95; a seat staggered at REACH start with its walk command at tick 54 clears at 91; a seat staggered mid-walk at tick 60 has the 30 frozen ticks counted, and the fixture asserts the resulting hit/clear outcome per draw rather than a clearance; carrier outcomes per draw match 11 of 18 (61.1 %) clear at `UNFOLD_TICKS >= 130`.
- **AC-HE-89 (auto) HE-F6, HE-F14, HE-F10, HE-F12.** Speed tables for 1000 and 1500 as in AC-HE-49; `V_HUNT_MPX` 2000 fails; `ceil(V_MPX / 1000) <= 3`; `72 < 96 < 192` and `96 <= 3 × 53 = 159` (a load with `REACH_RANGE_PX` 160 or 72 fails); expansions at most 14 on the slice; `S_h` never exceeds 2.
- **AC-HE-90 (auto) HE-F7, HE-F9.** Retention 1824 ticks with 5 non-smear marks and 1536 with 8; `MARK_LIFE_TICKS < smear_retention_ticks` fails the load; the ingress timeline of AC-HE-42, `INGRESS_DELAY_TICKS` 400 fails, `D_entry_max` 492.
- **AC-HE-91 (auto) HE-F8, HE-F13, HE-F15, HE-F17, HE-F18.** The five target cases (A lit 1000 against B dark 0 selects A; A lamp off selects ANCHOR; a Chemlight pool reading 700 is never a target while a seat standing in it is a candidate; two lit at 1000 and 1000 select the lower seat id; none lit selects ANCHOR); for `tell_len` 600 the first strike is no earlier than 760 ticks after TELL entry and `layered_warning_min >= W_MIN_WARNING_S × TICK_RATE` (600), and for `tell_len` 1800 no later than 2045; a stationary seat's strike-to-strike interval is `UNFOLD_TICKS + 365` (460 to 545 ticks, `min_gap` 395 after a fizzle, `t_out` 96 ≤ `STRIKE_COOLDOWN_TICKS`); `alpha5` is 0.5 at two ticks after the run with `tick_alpha` 0.5, and the rig pose never changes between runs.
- **AC-HE-92 (auto, exact enumeration) HE-F16.** GIVEN a stub `rng_int` and all 18 × 18 = 324 pairs of `UNFOLD_TICKS` draws for the first two REACHes of a stationary lit seat (`s_3 = 795 + U_1 + U_2`), WHEN the share of pairs with `s_3 < B` is counted, THEN it is 0 of 324 at B = 900, 1 of 324 at B = 990 (0.3 %), 6 of 324 at 1000 (1.9 %), 91 of 324 at 1050 (28.1 %), 246 of 324 at 1100 (75.9 %), 323 of 324 at 1155 (99.7 %) and 324 of 324 at B >= 1156; the mean `T_3` is exactly 1207.5 ticks (range 1080 to 1335). A 1000-seed Monte Carlo is kept only as a **(spike)**, never a pass/fail rule (a ±0.03 tolerance would false-fail about 3 % of runs).

### Group X: cross-system criteria (BLOCKING)

- **AC-HE-93 (auto, integration) Attention's must-repeat list, item by item.** GIVEN Attention's seven must-repeat items, WHEN each is checked, THEN each is answered: the hook is `after: [attention.step, emitter.place]` (AC-HE-10); no exogenous harm outside BREACH or SUBSIDING in the locus body, asserted through `tell_window_contract` with CHILL excluded (AC-HE-1 to 4, 7); no lethal act on a crew body outside it (AC-HE-5); it honours `TellAudit` (AC-HE-4); the per-window budget (AC-HE-55); the single MVP behaviour is wired to BREACH (**BLOCKING**, AC-HE-12) and a non-lethal presence outside BREACH (**BLOCKING**, AC-HE-14); the suppressant per AT-19 (AC-HE-73); L3 as state (AC-HE-65). *Covers AC-AT-77.*
- **AC-HE-94 (auto, integration) Graph.** GIVEN a GOUGE, WHEN the Graph runs, THEN the segment is `fatigued` and its creep sits under the Graph's own `W_MIN_WARNING_S` gate (Horror neither alters nor shortens it); GIVEN a RUPTURE on a gouged segment, THEN the Graph raises its own told consequence (descriptor and breach clock per LSG) and Horror's call is Class B from `horror.step` and never a `SYSTEM` command. *Covers HE-16, HE-28.* *(provisional — the RUPTURE outcome line to be cited from LSG on the confirming run.)*
- **AC-HE-95 (auto, integration) Suit.** GIVEN `apply_suit_damage`, WHEN called, THEN it is the 3-argument form; the class travels on `source_ref`, classed EXOGENOUS once at record creation (boot or ingress arrival), persisted with the record, and valid across restore independently of E6; an unclassed ref is a programmer error that fails the build; Horror reads no integrity value and no Suit rung. *Covers HE-15, AC-SE-41.*
- **AC-HE-96 (review + manual) Failure & Trail.** GIVEN a RUPTURE trail descriptor (along the rib or seam) and a Horror hairline mark (radial, no hole, no stroke below 2 px), WHEN both are shown, THEN they are never confused (AC-HE-110); Horror draws no Graph descriptor and F&T draws no Horror mark. *Covers FT-31.*
- **AC-HE-97 (review) Owed quotes.** GIVEN Interaction and Derelict Authoring, WHEN reviewed, THEN each quotes `HORROR_SUBJECTS_MAX` 2 and not 12 or 16 (C-HE-4, closed 2026-10-27), and IA-31 quotes `SUIT_EXO_BYPASS_LIMITER` 1 (C-HE-5, closed 2026-10-27).
- **AC-HE-98 (lint + auto) Body.** GIVEN the Horror source, WHEN scanned, THEN there is no `AGENT` row for a horror, no `CharacterBody2D`, no physics query and no `NavigationServer2D`; movement is only through `LocomotionStep` with the foot box `FOOT_BOX_W` 16 by `FOOT_BOX_H` 8; a fixture in which a seat's body overlaps the 64 to 96 px sprite extent but not the foot box shows the seat neither blocked nor struck; the reads are `actors_on`, `pos_curr`, `actor_section` and `loco_state`, none per tick. *Covers HE-23, HE-33.*
- **AC-HE-99 (auto) Layout reads.** GIVEN a run, WHEN reads are counted, THEN `room_adjacency` is read on a cache miss only, `room_at`, `anchor_geometry` and `solid_in` (at most 5) per run, `body_of` and `sections_of`; none per tick; the hull-segment lookup and `emitters_near` are present or the criteria that need them read NOT ASSESSED. *Covers HE-25, HE-F10.* *(provisional — Layout/Graph and Emitter/Lighting reads owed, C-HE-11, C-HE-15.)*
- **AC-HE-100 (spike + manual) Pipeline draw groups.** GIVEN `MAX_MARKS` 24 marks in view, WHEN rendered, THEN Horror draw groups never exceed `HORROR_MARK_DRAW_GROUPS` 4. The guarantee at the minimum `D_marks` (4 to 6, where the knob table gives Horror none) is **NOT ASSESSED** until Pipeline and Failure & Trail rule on C-HE-31. *Covers HE-30.*
- **AC-HE-101 (lint) Sanity.** GIVEN the Horror source, WHEN scanned, THEN it holds no sanity reference, reads no `PERCEPTION_OUT` block and neither defines nor uses `HORROR_NEAR_R` or `EXP_HORROR`. *Covers HE-32, HE-40.* *(provisional — Sanity & Hallucination Rules designed in this run.)*
- **AC-HE-102 (review) Open Questions carry the corrections.** GIVEN this document, WHEN reviewed, THEN Open Questions names RUPTURE in the anchor ladder and `RETURN_HOME` bound to validation target (2), and every `(provisional — …)` registration names its owner.

- **AC-HE-123 (review, BLOCKING gate) Art-bible refresh before the rig's asset-spec.** GIVEN a request to run `/asset-spec system:horror-entities` for the rig (H1, H2), WHEN the owed rows ART:542 ("Physical honesty"), ART 5.5 intro, PIPE:140 ("horror" removed from the occluder casters) and IDX:35 bullet 18 are checked, THEN each carries the no-occluder, no-shadow wording of HE-2 and the fiction gloss, or the asset-spec is refused; the rime and hairline sheets (H4, H5, H7) additionally need C-HE-21 decided. This is a precondition, not advice. *Covers HE-2, C-HE-1, C-HE-21.*

If the user reverses AT-19 the effect and the item arrive together. *Covers HE-39, C-HE-6.*

### Group Z: performance and soak

- **AC-HE-103 (spike) Per-run cost.** GIVEN a slice with one horror, 4 seats and a full mark ring on the minimum-spec reference machine (the platform config's minimum, to be named in the QA plan), WHEN `horror.step` is profiled over 10000 runs, THEN P99 is at most `HORROR_STEP_BUDGET_MS` 0.15; the GDScript cost, the rig draw count (10 to 13 sprites in the Y-sorted layer) and per-instance shader parameters are spike parameters because `docs/engine-reference/godot/` has no `scripting.md`, `core.md` or 2D-skeleton coverage.
- **AC-HE-104 (auto) Per-run counts.** GIVEN any run, THEN `lux` reads ≤ 4, `solid_in` queries ≤ 5, expansions ≤ `PATH_EXPANSIONS_MAX` 64, probes 0, mark inserts ≤ `MARKS_PER_RUN_MAX` 2, M17 calls ≤ `MAX_DEPOSITS_PER_RUN` 4, `apply_suit_damage` ≤ 1 per seat, dirty keys per tick ≤ `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256; and the serialised `horror.state` with a full ring, 4 L3 entries and 16 `last_end_T` entries is bounded (the bound is set from the spike).
- **AC-HE-105 (auto, soak) Repeated cycles.** GIVEN an accelerated 4-hour-equivalent simulation of repeated breach cycles, THEN the block size is constant, every `*_withheld` counter is 0, no `mark_id` is reused and a replay `state_hash` matches.

### Group M: legibility, feel and the visual half (BLOCKING; retained screenshots; lead sign-off)

**Validation targets** (Player Fantasy; creative-director, placeholders). (1) *Fair death:* at least 80 % of horror-caused deaths are described with a cause the player saw; below 60 % the telegraph or stalk is failing. (2) *Not an alarm monster:* in at least 70 % of sessions the player sees the horror or its fresh marks during CALM at least once. (3) *The dilemma exists:* at least half of testers asked "what did you do when it came?" describe turning the lamp off, abandoning a repair or closing a door. (4) *No hidden-number belief:* zero describe the horror "remembering" or "locking on" across a lamp-off. (5) *Marks read as web, not ship:* blind classification of rime against coolant frost is correct at least 90 % at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2`. (6) *Teeth:* in a replay of a breach on a clean ship the player loses at least one repair's worth of progress or one suit rung in at least 60 % of the breaches they choose to commit through lit. (7) *A CALM sighting changes behaviour:* at least 60 % of testers who saw the horror or a fresh mark in CALM report that it changed what they did (route, lamp, or which job first); if it fails, flip `RETURN_HOME` to 0 first and never relax HE-11 (AC-HE-122). **Sample rule:** every percentage threshold below needs N ≥ 10 testers (N ≥ 10 sessions for telemetry rows); a shorter run reads NOT ASSESSED. These rows are run first in playtest because they decide whether the numeric rows matter. **Evidence:** `production/qa/evidence/horror-entities/<ac-id>/`.

- **AC-HE-106 (manual, blind) Blind legibility of the telegraph (validation target 1).** GIVEN N >= 10 first-time players each experiencing a told breach with a lit seat and the horror approaching from behind the lamp cone, WHEN the REACH reaches `reach_end_T`, THEN the limb tip is visibly lit for the victim in every captured frame in all five facings at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2` (the tip rests on P inside the victim's own light); at least 87.5 % (7 of 8 equivalent) name the limb or the smear as the cause of a hit or a miss; and at least 75 % of those hit call it avoidable. If this fails the fallback is a larger `UNFOLD_LO` or a smaller `R_SNAP_PX` (first tweak 48), never a marker. *Covers HE-14, HE-F5, C-HE-9.*
- **AC-HE-107 (manual) The tip at the narrowest field.** GIVEN a seat aiming away from the horror at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2` (including 1080p 384 × 216), WHEN a REACH is in the hold stage, THEN the tip at the seat's foot is on screen at all three, and body and limb visibility is recorded per step; at k = 1 only "limb extended" is required. *Covers HE-F14, C-HE-23.*
- **AC-HE-108 (manual) Silhouette and lit frame.** GIVEN the poses `rest`, `reach`, `move` and `snap`, WHEN shown at 25 % with no colour and no contour, THEN each passes the art-bible silhouette test and contains no closed circle, bilateral symmetry, four-limb pairs or held object; a Void Steel rig on a lit Graphite floor reads as a figure under each of the four beam shapes and the three ring ambients with an achromatic pass; and a blind tester separates a real still frame from a stage-2 "shadow with no occluder" in at least 15 of 20 trials (binomial p < 0.05 against 50 %) with hue removed. *Covers HE-40, ART 3.1, ART 4.4.*
- **AC-HE-109 (manual, blind) Collision fixtures (validation target 5).** GIVEN FT AC-79 (frost fan against branching rime, Chemlight pool against rime, O2 mist against smear, RUPTURE trail against hairlines), WHEN shown in greyscale single frames at k = 1, `k_auto` and `k_auto + 2` to N ≥ 10, THEN each pair is classified correctly at least 90 %, the lit rendered rime measures L\* 70 to 78 and hue at least 36 degrees from flesh, and the art-director signs off. *Covers HE-31.*
- **AC-HE-110 (manual) Hairlines are not trails.** GIVEN a STRIKE within `HULL_CONTACT_PX`, WHEN the result is shown, THEN radial hairlines with no hole at the centre and no stroke below 2 px sit at the base of the hull face while Failure & Trail's descriptor runs along the rib or seam; the two never look alike. *Covers AC-HE-96.*
- **AC-HE-111 (manual, blind) The three "edge of my light" sources.** GIVEN a room with a TELL in progress and a horror present, WHEN N ≥ 10 testers watch, THEN at least 80 % separate "the lamp near the thing" (L3: local, irregular), "the dips on the beat" (Attention: global) and "the ship's power" (a Graph sag) by rhythm alone. *Covers HE-35.*
- **AC-HE-112 (manual + lint) Ingress and no fade.** GIVEN an ingress, egress, ageing and eviction, WHEN a 60 fps capture is stepped, THEN every Horror sprite alpha is 0 or 255 in every frame (lint: no tween on `modulate:a` in the presenter); where a wall face exists the rig emerges across its south edge; the ingress mark precedes it. *Covers V8, V9, V14.*
- **AC-HE-113 (manual) Reduced motion and reduced flashing.** GIVEN each accessibility setting on, WHEN the telegraph plays, THEN the unfold-hold-snap sequence is present and readable; no Horror luminance change exceeds 3 Hz (method: a 10 s capture at 60 fps, count luminance sign changes of the Horror sprite and of any L3-dipped lamp, at most 6 per second); the achromatopsia and colourblind passes keep the telegraph (shape, motion, stillness).
- **AC-HE-114 (manual, telemetry) CALM presence (validation target 2).** GIVEN N ≥ 10 thirty-minute solo sessions, WHEN telemetry is read, THEN in at least 70 % the player saw the horror or a fresh mark in CALM at least once (seen: on screen at least 1 s, logged as an event) and the horror never chose a step below `LURK_STANDOFF_PX`. If it fails, flip `RETURN_HOME` first. *Covers HE-11, HE-20.*
- **AC-HE-115 (manual, telemetry) Teeth and the dilemma (validation targets 3, 4, 6).** GIVEN a replay of a breach on a clean ship, WHEN N ≥ 10 breaches that the player chose to commit through lit are counted (lamp on, in the locus body at BREACH entry, channelling a lit-only verb), THEN in at least 60 % the player loses one completed channel's progress or one suit rung; at least half of testers asked "what did you do when it came?" describe turning the lamp off, abandoning a repair or closing a door; zero describe it "remembering" or "locking on" across a lamp-off. *Covers HE-12, HE-13, HE-26.*
- **AC-HE-116 (manual) Game Feel: the strike is witnessed by the strike.** GIVEN a blind playtest of N ≥ 10, WHEN horror-caused deaths are described, THEN at least 80 % are described with a cause the player saw; strike one is witnessed by the snap pose, Audio's positional cue from `strike_T` and the STRUCK mark on the floor (a tester who returns to the spot reads it as "it got me here"); there is no hit-stop, screen shake, damage number or flash from any Horror source. *Covers HE-15, HE-38, validation target (1), Game Feel.*
- **AC-HE-122 (manual, telemetry or interview) A CALM sighting changes behaviour (validation target 7).** GIVEN N ≥ 10 thirty-minute solo sessions, WHEN testers who saw the horror or a fresh mark in CALM are asked on one neutral question "did seeing it change what you did next?", THEN at least 60 % report a change in route, lamp use or which job they took first because of it. If it fails, flip `RETURN_HOME` to 0 first (OQ-1); CALM stays non-lethal (AC-HE-14) and HE-11 is not relaxed. *Covers HE-11, HE-20, Overview, Player Fantasy.*
- **AC-HE-117 (manual + auto) Presenter cadence.** GIVEN a 60 fps capture, WHEN stepped, THEN the rig pose changes only on ticks that are multiples of 5, segment rotation is a multiple of 15 degrees in every captured frame, position is interpolated and pose is not. *Covers HE-40, HE-F18.*
- **AC-HE-118 (manual) Dark, restore, unlit marks.** GIVEN marks and rig at 0 % ambient with no light in range, THEN neither is drawn (the hull-floor tint is not applied); the first frame after a restore into LURK, STALK, REACH (mid-hold) and RETREAT shows the correct pose and mark set with no transition and, for REACH, the full remaining telegraph. *Covers D-AD1, V19.*
- **AC-HE-119 (manual, UI) No Horror surface in the HUD.** GIVEN the HUD states captured by the HUD owner (default, each accessibility setting, lamp on and off, sanity-degraded) with a horror in view, WHEN screenshots are taken of each, THEN none shows a horror, a threat bar, a proximity indicator or a direction, and a lint finds no HUD asset naming a horror. *Covers UI Requirements, AT-24.* *(HUD & Suit Layer designed (`hud-and-suit-layer.md`); the state list is finalised by its UX spec.)*
- **AC-HE-120 (lint) Stale-token scan.** GIVEN this GDD and every file that cites it (Attention, Emitter, Lighting, Suit, Interaction, Failure & Trail, Item DB, Derelict Authoring, Save/Load), WHEN scanned whole-word and case-sensitive against the list at the end of Cross-References with a whitelist file for quotations and negations ("near" meaning within 60 characters), THEN there are zero hits outside the whitelist. The exemption is checked by review. *Covers the scan list.*

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **The CHILL degrade path (C-HE-28).** "CHILL withheld outside BREACH and SUBSIDING" is a contingency with no knob and no rule; its criterion is NOT ASSESSED until the Attention owner rules AT:373's prose literal or narrows it.
- **`HORROR_RESTORE_RNG_COUNTER` (C-HE-30).** The condition that makes the counter invalid is not defined; AC-HE-77 proposes "negative or non-integer" so the code has one crafted save.
- **`D_marks` 4 to 6 (C-HE-31).** The knob table gives Horror zero draw groups there; no guarantee is assertable until Pipeline and Failure & Trail rule (AC-HE-100).
- **Branch-angle spacing (C2).** The art-director's "no two adjacent spacings within 5 degrees" has no source in HE-31 or FT:1427; only seed purity is asserted.
- **L3 duration versus the multiple-of-5 lint (C-HE-29).** HE-4 now exempts `L3_DUR_LO` and `L3_DUR_HI`; AC-HE-65 asserts the 18 to 30 range.
- **Provisional rows.** AC-HE-30 to 33 and 99 wait on the hull-segment lookup (C-HE-11); AC-HE-38 and 48 on Modular Ship Assembly; AC-HE-63, 64 and 74 (camera jolt) on the Perception Filter and Pipeline; AC-HE-71 on Adaptive Audio; AC-HE-101 on Sanity; AC-HE-119 on the HUD UX spec. Each reads NOT ASSESSED until its neighbour exists.
- **Reference hardware.** AC-HE-103 needs the minimum-spec machine named in the QA plan.
- **Manual sample sizes.** Group M percentages assume N ≥ 10; the minimum N and the result when N is short (NOT ASSESSED) are stated once above the group.
- **Hold-stage pose count at the lower bound.** The art-director's "last 30 % of the unfold, not under 27 ticks" holds at `UNFOLD_LO` 95 (28.5 ticks, 6 poses); it is asserted by AC-HE-106 and the presenter stub of the Visual/Audio section, not by a separate row.

## Open Questions

Owner: **user** = a creative or scope decision the user (via the creative-director) must make; **agent** = a specialist or neighbour-GDD author; **GDD** = this document's author. Target: **playtest**, **next GDD** (the named neighbour), **ADR** (an architecture decision). Rows 1 to 7 are the user-decision items; the autonomous run proposed the answer shown and proceeded on it. `[CUT-FIRST]` marks an item that is the first thing to cut if scope tightens.

|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`RETURN_HOME` and validation target (2).** Default 1: the horror returns to derelict B after a breach, so a CALM sighting needs the player to be near it. The creative-director leans to 0 (it lurks where it last breached). If fewer than 70 % of 30-minute solo sessions see the horror or a fresh mark in CALM (AC-HE-114), flip this knob first. Ruled at the CD-GDD-ALIGN gate: keep default 1 for MVP (a home body on derelict B maps risk to place, Pillar 5, and keeps the home-ship from being permanently haunted before playtest data exists); flip to 0 first if validation target (2) or the CALM behaviour-change target (7, AC-HE-122) fails | user | playtest |

Should the dark-seat answer also risk a RUPTURE after a GOUGE? Proposed: no at MVP | user | playtest |
